Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the metabolism framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 623. The resulting operator achieves bound O(791/n^4).

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_623(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 623. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^791(S, F) is trivial. QED.
